# Ideas left inventory, 2026-10-25

Status: D5 proposal inventory, source-crosswalk complete at this snapshot. This is a crosswalk to existing owners, not a second backlog or build authorization. Weibao keeps every idea on the table; unplayed work needs presentation, not removal ([direction](../USER_VISION.md#first-milestone-and-scope-check-2026-10-24)).

Rules applied: AD5/6–8/22/32–38/41; WPR1–8/18–25/27–32; AU5/10–14; dispatch M1–M9. Docs only in the primary checkout. No commit, merge, push, runtime edit, or acceptance claim.

Existing authority: [systems register](../SYSTEMS_REGISTER.md), its [making/items/economy](../systems/MAKING_ITEMS_ECONOMY.md) and [progression/world/state](../systems/PROGRESSION_WORLD_STATE.md) entries; [MAW-X1–X9](../gm/MECHANIC_ABSORPTION_WORKFLOW.md#expansion-backlog-keep-growing-this-2026-10-25). Their codes and detail remain owned there.


Read: [system/world summary](#summary-by-system-and-world), [full crosswalk](#full-crosswalk-table), [unlabelled proposals](#unlabelled-palette-and-nested-owner-remainders), [chat ideas never recorded](#chat-ideas-never-recorded), [ranked next buildable slices](#ranked-next-buildable-slices), [coverage and checks](#source-coverage-and-deduplication-ledger).

Coverage: 46 root/directly indexed palette owners plus 37 nested filename routes; all 136 audit/review Markdown paths dispositioned; 30 world cards plus world-depth, BF, institution and progression owners. Chat review covers 1,669 selected user records, 1,409 sanitized personal-message bodies, 36,195 words across 19 bounded chunks; 260 wrapper/pasted/empty records were filtered. Two exact chat scenarios are retained below, one with an unidentified source and one whose primitives existed but exact scenario was not explicit. Source census/outline routing is distinguished from semantic and runtime verification.

## Scope and evidence

Snapshot started at primary HEAD `3dfc49cf3422c0262d86810df47459f53f3a4a67`, with concurrent dirty runtime and documentation. Other lanes retain their owned work. D5 owns this report and one additive link each in IDEA_INDEX and SYSTEMS_REGISTER. Those two paths were clean at initial inspection. Extraction uses four disjoint Luna-low jobs: root palettes/index; audits/reviews; world cards/vision; user-only chat transcripts. No additional workers reserved. Included allowance telemetry unavailable; no API or paid fallback used.

State terms: **built and played** requires a located rebuilt play receipt at its stated scope, not human acceptance; **built and unverified** means source exists without such a receipt; **partial** means a bounded implementation exists but the named idea is broader; **proposal** means design without located delivery evidence; **blocked** names a specific missing prerequisite. “Proposal” is not proof that no code exists. Technical verification, integration, app adoption and director acceptance remain separate.

## Current state spot-checks

F1: The register's no-live-caller statement is stale in the current dirty tree: `src/crafting_frontier_runtime.js:14,137` imports and calls `matchRecipe`. `src/craft_recipes.js:79–120` returns boolean admission, assignment and unknown/problems. A concurrent bounded graded-fit implementation now exists in `src/craft_material_fit.js` and `src/native_rendering_recipes.js`: authored oil contribution changes native cooking quantities. Its active reference-mechanics checkpoint still requires the final playable receipt. MAW-X4 / MIE-G2 retain broader formula/potency/risk grading; do not dispatch either a duplicate caller or a duplicate native rendering slice.

F2: `src/progression_gates.js:43–44` validates overflow descriptors; validation alone does not establish runtime consumption. Keep the register's overflow gap open until a named consumer and outcome journey are located.

F3: Cultivation CG01–CG29 and all-reference absorption already have active [cultivation](../gm/dispatch/cultivation-absorption-2026-10-24/CHECKPOINT.md) and [reference](../gm/dispatch/reference-mechanics-2026-10-25/CHECKPOINT.md) owners. Do not redispatch their reserved implementations. The [C5 reconciliation](../gm/RECONCILIATION_2026-10-25.md) separates branch proof, master integration, current dirty state and U1–U4 repair ownership.




## Summary by system and world

This table is navigation, not a second specification. The full crosswalk below retains source codes and records both feature proposals and constraints. Repeated short codes are qualified by their source path; a shared code range means the named owner retains each member's detail. No idea is dropped because it is large, unplayed, paused or not selected.

| System | Existing idea families and remaining scope | Current disposition |
|---|---|---|
| Making / items / trade | MIE-G1–G14, MAW-X3/X4, PZ/TSW, GDP/ESD, procedural recipes, lineage, decay, currencies, escrow, traders, tools and maker arts | Base pipelines exist; current reference/cultivation work is reserved. Generic grading, cross-world adapters and material/quality depth remain partial/proposed. |
| Growth / learning / gates | PROGRESSION_WORLD_STATE:G1–G4/G10/G13/G14; SLR, GTO, path shapes, practice, overflow, teaching and niche specialties | Existing skill overflow is not a universal gate consumer. Per-world gate palettes preserve distinct mechanisms and evidence. |
| Lives / bodies / cheats | Continuation, baggage, selves, recovery, high-tier means, CHEAT_LIBRARY IM family, body and health, genetics | Many bounded native slices exist; current cultivation and D4 own continuation/cheat deltas. Shared custody, body/knowledge boundaries and variants need exact-owner evidence. |
| People / homes / institutions | Companion, relationship, party, household, BF lives, roles, law and daily-life palettes | Native finite loops do not implement all world roles. Ordinary work, personal story, relationships and niche paths remain on the table. |
| Places / travel / worlds | SR, W4 K5/K9/K13/K14, world-depth 01–24/28–41/43, route/repair/service loops | Cards and research floors are not playable-world completion. Retain source/era/start and pause distinctions. |
| UI / player experience | UIA/DL/L residuals, ID details placement, RUC/glow, every panel, empty/loading/disabled states, active/idle attention | D1 owns current overhaul, desktop first. Existing published/frozen UI proofs remain exact-input receipts; human taste is separate. |
| Engine / persistence / tooling | MAW-X1–X9, progression register G5–G9/G12, performance, host, pack saves, command migration, harnesses and agentic work | D2/D3/D6 and C2/C4 have live scopes. No language migration or new autonomous authority selected by this inventory. |
| Reference and source work | Acquisition/edition gaps, deeper systems and mechanically distinct worlds | Source reading, authored proposals, native code, played proof and normal-app adoption remain separate. No source purchase, private export or account action authorized here. |

World coverage retains the 30-card census and source-specific extension owners. Canon-first cards include Harry Potter, D&D, TTKS books 1–5, Mother of Learning, LOTM/COI, Shadow Slave, Reincarnation Paradise, JJK, Bleach, Fate, Hunter×Hunter, Fullmetal Alchemist and Naruto. Original Frontier and Mistglass have bounded native journeys; Soldier's Life retains its current pause. Primordial Saint Sect and Regressor's Tale retain partial authoring; newer Re:Zero, Tensura, Worm, Perfect Run, Super Supportive, You Are Summoned, Godclads and Wish Upon the Stars source work does not become built gameplay. The world tables preserve each owner's narrower codes and remaining source/implementation checks.

## Full crosswalk table

Locator key: `cards/` means `docs/worlds/world-core-kit-2026-10-14/cards/`; `W4_PLACES_CRAFT_LOOP_LOOK.md` is in its parent directory; `WORLD_CORE_KIT_2026-10-14.md` and its successor are under `docs/worlds/`. Unprefixed root palette names mean `docs/`. Lines refer to the extraction snapshot and may move as other lanes edit.

The table is organized by existing owner rather than inventing another backlog hierarchy. Suggested levels are Codex equivalents: Luna-low for extraction/clerical work, Luna-medium for bounded state checking, Sol-medium for design, synthesis, code and playable verification. Historic source suggestions such as Sonnet/Haiku/Opus remain provenance; they do not mandate those models. Where the source has no estimate, effort is explicitly unset until a bounded slice is chosen. A source-only **proposal** row means no delivery proof was established for that row in this pass, not proof that no implementation exists anywhere.

| PF2 | **Objects have useful qualities beyond their category.** / Start with properties a real consumer reads; add scale, condition, shape or supernatural traits when an interaction needs them. A tag summarizes behavior, not permission to invent any matching outcome. / A heavy iron pot can contain liquid, collect rain or hold down a cloth. Its weight and open mouth matter; a sealed vial behaves differently. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:213 | proposal | PF2; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF2; no standalone feature |
| PF3 | **Separate intent from method.** / Offer a concrete goal with available methods where useful. World law and actual means decide whether each method can achieve it. Some special locks may demand an exact key or oath. / Open a stuck hatch by leverage, softening a swollen frame, removing hinges or a supported spell. Several methods can exist without flattening their noise, time or damage. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:214 | proposal | PF3; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF3; no standalone feature |
| PF4 | **Address a part or condition of the target.** / Use a few meaningful parts or states, not full physical simulation. Increase detail for activities where it creates decisions. / Dry a book before reading it; loosen a handle instead of breaking a whole door; coat a blade's edge rather than treat its sheath. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:215 | proposal | PF4; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF4; no standalone feature |
| PF5 | **Preparation and environment are usable inputs.** / Temperature, light, moisture, support and timing matter where the local process reads them. Add no mandatory preparation ritual where it has no effect. / Warm a stiff mixture, brace a tool or work by daylight. Rain may supply water while making exposed ink work harder. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:216 | proposal | PF5; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF5; no standalone feature |
| PF6 | **Substitution is about function and local law.** / Permit a substitute when it supplies the required role; preserve exact identities when a world makes those significant. Quality and side effects follow the actual substitute, without a compulsory surcharge. / A clean bowl replaces a cup for mixing, but a rite that binds a named heirloom still needs that heirloom or a real means to alter the rite. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:217 | proposal | PF6; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF6; no standalone feature |
| PF7 | **Amount, concentration and scale can change a use.** / Distinguish quantities only where a consumer makes them matter. Let the activity decide whether splitting, dilution, combining or scaling is useful, with declared conservation or explicit creation means. / A drop stains a page, a coating treats an edge, and a full basin washes a garment. Dilution may spread an effect while weakening it. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:218 | proposal | PF7; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF7; no standalone feature |
| PF8 | **Effects can matter outside their advertised purpose.** / Expose selected physical or magical consequences to neighboring systems. Extend the chain when there is a useful consumer, rather than attaching every tag to every system. / A cooling charm preserves food or eases a fever when those processes support cooling; it need not become a universal healing spell. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:219 | proposal | PF8; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF8; no standalone feature |
| PF9 | **Knowledge guides a use without creating the underlying property.** / Ordinary handling stays available through ordinary means; special procedures can require real knowledge, control or apparatus. Unknown results remain uncertain rather than inert. / An unidentified adhesive still sticks. Experimenting may reveal it, waste some or create a residue the character can later investigate. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:220 | proposal | PF9; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF9; no standalone feature |
| PF10 | **Progress can be partial and failures can leave usable things.** / Retain meaningful work and actual byproducts where the process supports them. Irreversible destruction, full recovery or no useful lesson are also possible locally. / A failed forging leaves a bent fitting, salvage or a diagnostic fracture; an interrupted translation retains the understood passage. No guaranteed failure XP or refund. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:221 | partial | PF10; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF10; no standalone feature |
| PF11 | **An obstacle has more than one kind of boundary.** / Keep physical feasibility, present readiness, knowledge, social permission and implementation coverage distinct. Institutions may object without making an act physically impossible. / A locked archive can be inaccessible, forbidden, unlocated or simply not implemented, for different reasons. A forged pass affects the guard's belief, not the door's metal. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:222 | built and unverified | PF11; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF11; no standalone feature |
| PF12 | **Means can be combined, borrowed or supplied as a service.** / Combine actual contributions with their scope and custody; extend cooperation when its domain is selected. A machine, hired service or existing simple actor route can supply a missing step without a new NPC simulation. / You know the design but rent the kiln. A tool guides your hand without granting permanent mastery. | PF13 | **Changes can create opportunities as well as costs.** / Preserve meaningful aftermath with an actual reader. Ordinary use need not attract automatic suspicion, punishment or a balancing counter. / Broken masonry supplies rubble; a drained basin exposes a mark; a successful demonstration creates a saleable sample. Whether anyone notices depends on their means. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:224 | proposal | PF13; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF13; no standalone feature |
| PF14 | **Discovered combinations become reusable methods.** / Save the successful procedure and its demonstrated conditions; a shortcut rechecks tools, supply and scope. Changeable substitutions stay visible where useful. / A discovered way to dry herbs becomes a routine, not an invented new skill tree or a guaranteed recipe for every herb and climate. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:225 | proposal | PF14; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF14; no standalone feature |
| PF15 | **Local impossibility need not close the wider journey.** / A world's law can be absolute within its scope. Other bodies, tools, worlds or rule-changing cheats can offer routes when their actual means support them. Do not invent a secret workaround merely to satisfy a menu. / A hand cannot touch a spirit; a compatible vessel or technique may let the player interact. Finding such a means is a possible pursuit, not an automatically revealed checklist. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:226 | proposal | PF15; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: little choices with large consequences; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF15; no standalone feature |

A superior faculty can legitimately do several at once. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:309 | proposal | PF27; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: ability details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF27; no standalone feature |

| PF33 | Transmission and records are usable outputs / A sound technique may carry a message, a memory faculty preserve a witnessed pattern, and an imprint hold an instruction. | PF35 | A sensor probe is a real action / A mirror around a corner, a dropped pebble, a held lamp or a remote sense supplies particular evidence. Repeated identical evidence need not teach more. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:322 | proposal | PF35; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF35; no standalone feature |
| PF36 | Local conditions can be changed / Move an obstruction, open a shutter, drain an authored basin or support a loose beam. The resulting state should affect an actual route, reading, resource or shelter function. | PF37 | Information can lead before it confirms / A copied map, measured bearing or remembered landmark can guide a first approach. Staleness, coordinate frame and actual source lineage matter. Correct directions can reach an entrance without surveying every interior or granting permission. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:324 | proposal | PF37; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF37; no standalone feature |
| PF38 | Field aids have placement and custody / An anchored rope, chalk mark or portable lamp occupies an actual place and can help a later traversal. Preserve forgiving recovery direction; do not introduce an undisclosed permanent trap as a small exploration detail. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:326 | proposal | PF39; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF39; no standalone feature |

A copy preserves its actual channel and detail, not all properties or powers of the original. Ordinary inspection remains a free preview; sampling is a distinct quoted action if it consumes material. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:327 | proposal | PF40; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF40; no standalone feature |
| PF41 | Travel can change what is carried / An exposed record gets wet, a glowing object illuminates a route, and a large body fits different passages when those processes exist. Conditions attach to actual instances or batches, rather than rewriting an entire item template. Ordinary travel adds no unsupported wear or chores. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:328 | proposal | PF41; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF41; no standalone feature |
| PF42 | Return has persistent practical meaning / A cleared drain remains clear until a declared cause changes it; installed shelter can later support rest. Visits do not reroll stock or advance paused worlds. A place can stay unchanged and still be useful. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:329 | proposal | PF42; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: exploration details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF42; no standalone feature |
| PF43 | Carrying and containing are different services / A rigid case protects pages, a sling frees a hand and a bucket holds liquid. A container's functions can matter without making all inventories a packing puzzle. Extra-dimensional storage follows its own actual access and preservation rules. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:335 | proposal | PF43; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: everyday action details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF43; no standalone feature |
| PF44 | Clean, dry, warm and mend are ordinary verbs / Cleaning can reveal a readable surface, drying restore a usable record, warming loosen wax and mending restore containment. Select condition transitions with consumers; no mandatory hygiene meter or daily deterioration loop. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:336 | proposal | PF44; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: everyday action details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF44; no standalone feature |
| PF45 | Body and tool fit change the method / A large handle, alternate grip, beak-held tool or remote manipulator can support the same intent differently. Hand count is relevant to an action that uses hands, not a global prerequisite for ordinary agency. Adapted equipment or powers can replace the usual method. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:337 | proposal | PF45; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: everyday action details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF45; no standalone feature |

| PF47 | Preparation can be economical and pleasant / Packing a known kit, choosing a warm seat or preparing a sheltered workspace may reduce actual setup time or exposure. No bonus, fee, disadvantage or minigame is required for every everyday action. | docs/PLAY_BEYOND_MENU_SELECTORS_CONSIDERATIONS_2026-10-14.md:339 | proposal | PF47; ACCOUNT FOR NOW: everyday action details; source status/check only | Sol, medium (if implementation) | constraint on owning slice | owning slice demonstrates compliance with PF47; no standalone feature |

## Nested research and dispatch palette/consideration routes

## Evidence and limits

- This coverage fill uses title census, section outlines, code families and named current-owner corrections; it avoids copying entire specs and avoids rereading all 46 owner bodies. The preceding D5 file preserves exact per-row source locators for every coded ACCOUNT/LATER candidate it found.
- “Proposal” is not acceptance. “Built and played” applies only to the bounded receipt named; it does not imply human acceptance, general feature-family completion, app adoption or release.
- Source rows with no code are grouped by exact heading locator. Their bounds are source sections, not invented stable IDs.


## Chat ideas never recorded



## Result

The full semantic pass found **one uncovered design/research question and one exact scenario missing from otherwise related owners**: a world system can be restrained or held back by a powerful character while still exerting indirect effects on the protagonist. The source was unidentified in the message, and I found no direct owner entry for that interaction. This remains an unverified proposal, not an accepted mechanic. The other substantive idea groups reviewed below already have owner coverage; I found no evidence that their chat phrasing asks for an unrecorded mechanic beyond those owners.

## Corpus and method

- Snapshot date: 2026-10-25. Claude corpus: 266 project JSONL files, 1,088 human external user messages. Codex corpus: 415 recursive session JSONL files; 223 sessions selected by exact `session_meta.payload.cwd == /Users/weiwei/code/yet-another-idle-rpg`, containing 581 user messages. Total: **1,669 user-message records**. No read errors.
- Extraction retained source path, JSONL line, timestamp, and session ID. Claude selection required `type=user`, exact repo cwd, `origin.kind=human`, and `userType=external`. Codex selection required exact session cwd and `response_item` message role `user`. Assistant messages and tool results were not read as user ideas.
- I reviewed the sanitized personal-message bodies in 19 chronological, bounded chunks, each at or below 2,000 words (36,195 words total). The cleaner removed ambient wrapper blocks, embedded pasted-content blocks, full agent-history dumps, and obvious dispatch/instruction payloads; where a mixed message had `## My request:`, only the request portion was retained. Every remaining candidate record was reviewed, not just keyword matches. The 260 records that did not enter those views were empty after filtering or consisted of ambient/pasted/agent-directed material; there are **no unread sanitized chunks or remaining ID ranges**.
- This is a semantic review, not proof of perfect interpretation: the corpus is large, some user-role entries embed copied context, and classification can miss a subtle idea even after a full pass. I excluded copied agent instructions and third-party suggestions as ideas authored by Weibao. Quotes below are short and selected to identify the idea, not to reproduce full messages. No sealed truth is included.
- The original aggregate JSON was stale by three Codex records; counts here use the extracted index (581 Codex, 1,669 total), not the older 578/1,666 summary.

## Source coverage and deduplication ledger

Four Luna-low extractors covered disjoint source families, followed by bounded repair passes within the same four jobs. The coordinator checked the high-consequence state claims against the current source and receipts: matcher caller versus grading, native rendering overlap, gate descriptor versus skill overflow, existing tool wear/repair versus typed damage, pack persistence versus registry projection, W1–W4 final native receipt, and C1 branch proof versus primary U3. Source-derived proposal rows were not individually runtime-tested. No whole current dirty-tree release was certified.

Source sizes count deterministic censuses, not a claim that every word was re-read semantically. Raw chat extracts and candidate text are temporary working data and are not copied into this report. Coverage limitations remain explicit below; absent evidence does not delete a possibility.

### Deduplication and freshness decisions

D1: Existing codes and owners win. MAW-X rows, MIE-G rows and progression-register G rows are linked crosswalks, not newly numbered work. G1–G14 are qualified by PROGRESSION_WORLD_STATE to avoid collision with unrelated G codes. World-card recurring short IDs are source-local.

D2: Historic open labels do not reopen a shipped finite slice. W1–W4 and IP1–IP3 remain implemented at their recorded scope. Broader world kits, tool damage/layers and human pacing are separate remainders. C3/C2 branch results supersede old playtest snapshots only at their exact repaired scopes; branch integration and current combined verification remain G1's gates.

D3: Preserve active ownership. Cultivation CG01–CG29, reference-mechanics rendering, C1 LOTM, C2 MCP, C3 playtest fixes, C4 test work, D1 UI, D2 engine, D4 cheats and D7 HP stay with their lanes. D3 stack and D6 agentic proposals stay proposals. This inventory authorizes no overlapping implementation.

D4: ACCOUNT FOR NOW rows constrain the corresponding slice; they are not hundreds of independent feature tickets. Unlabelled proposals are grouped by their owning code families. Source-only facts, audit procedures, completed historical checks and evidence bundles are not invented backlog items.

## More-to-consider round D5.1

ACT NOW (done): retained original owner codes; repaired stale matcher/tool/W1–W4 classifications; preserved broad world direction and unplayed work; added the requested index/register links without rewriting their owners.

ACCOUNT FOR NOW: a current dirty helper can supersede a register sentence without being integrated, played or accepted. Refresh owner, branch and file identities before G1 dispatches. Separate a missing mechanic from a missing demonstration: showing an existing loop may be the right next slice. The report's table is a snapshot, not a live lease on files.

LATER / DELEGATE: MAW-X2/X9 can add a repeatable freshness receipt for gap owners, source hashes and related delivery checkpoints. Suggested Sol-medium, small/medium, one lane; done check: each existing gap row points to its current owner and exact proof boundary, without creating another backlog or requiring every export to be a feature. MAW-X6 can reuse the two-harness acceptance gate above. No extra implementation selected.
